\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02617}02617\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Purpose:\ Calculates\ the\ norm\ of\ a\ 3D\ vector\ using\ a\ given\ metric\ tensor}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02618}02618\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ }}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02620}02620\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ ||r||\_metric\ =\ sqrt(r\string^T\ *\ metric\ *\ r)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02621}02621\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ where\ r\ is\ a\ 3D\ vector\ and\ metric\ is\ a\ 3x3\ symmetric\ matrix}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02622}02622\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02623}02623\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Parameters:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02624}02624\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ -\/\ r:\ 3D\ position\ vector\ (input)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02625}02625\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ -\/\ metric:\ 3x3\ metric\ tensor\ (input)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02626}02626\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Returns:\ Norm\ of\ the\ vector\ in\ the\ given\ metric\ (real*8)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02627}02627\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02628}02628\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Algorithm:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02629}02629\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ 1.\ Compute\ quadratic\ form:\ r\string^T\ *\ metric\ *\ r}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02630}02630\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ 2.\ Take\ square\ root\ with\ protection\ from\ roundoff\ errors}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02631}02631\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ 3.\ Return\ the\ norm}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02632}02632\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02633}02633\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Usage:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02634}02634\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ This\ function\ is\ used\ in\ periodic\ boundary\ condition\ calculations}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02635}02635\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ to\ determine\ the\ distance\ between\ lattice\ points\ in\ a\ general\ metric}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02636}02636\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{keyword}{function\ }norm\_metric(r,\ metric)}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02638}02638\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ real*8\ norm\_metric,\ r(3),\ metric(3,\ 3)}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02639}02639\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Calculate\ norm\ using\ metric\ tensor:\ ||r||\ =\ sqrt(r\string^T\ *\ metric\ *\ r)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02640}02640\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ norm\_metric\ =\ metric(1,\ 1)*r(1)*r(1)\ +\ metric(2,\ 2)*r(2)*r(2)\ +\ metric(3,\ 3)*r(3)*r(3)\&}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02641}02641\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \&\ +\ 2.d0*(metric(1,\ 2)*r(1)*r(2)\ +\ metric(1,\ 3)*r(1)*r(3)\ +\ metric(2,\ 3)*r(2)*r(3))}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02642}02642\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ norm\_metric\ =\ dsqrt(max(norm\_metric,\ 0.d0))\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Protection\ from\ roundoff\ errors}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02654}02654\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ 1.\ For\ each\ coordinate\ (x,\ y,\ z),\ there\ are\ three\ possible\ minimum\ positions:}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02658}02658\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ 2.\ This\ results\ in\ 27\ possible\ combinations\ (3\string^3)}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02661}02661\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Mathematical\ Background:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02662}02662\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ When\ computing\ distances\ in\ periodic\ systems,\ the\ minimum\ distance}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02663}02663\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ between\ two\ points\ can\ occur\ at\ different\ positions\ within\ the\ unit\ cell.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02664}02664\ \textcolor{comment}{!\ This\ subroutine\ explores\ all\ possible\ combinations\ to\ find\ the\ true\ minimum.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02665}02665\ \textcolor{comment}{!}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{allio_8f90_source_l02686}02686\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{!\ Generate\ all\ possible\ combinations\ of\ boundary\ conditions}}
